# Oracle — class playtest v0.1

**PROVISIONAL — exact numbers and acquisition are proposals for table testing.** Approved source books remain intact. The17 class names identify Base classes; Arch and Hero are later stages, with explicit exceptions below.

[All classes](../CLASS_PLAYTEST_INDEX.md) · [Shared test rules](../CLASS_PLAYTEST_RULES.md) · [Session log](../CLASS_PLAYTEST_SESSION_LOG.md)

[Preserved book and registry evidence](../../../data/playtest/class_sources/oracle.json) · [Normalized card data](../../../data/public/class_playtests/oracle.json)

## Class engine and setup

**Book setup (provisional engine resolves any explicit conflicts below):**

- Main Attribute: Charisma (determines Revelation potency, saves, aura strength)
- Secondary Attributes: Wisdom (for Curse triggers, insight, and resistances)
- Hit Points: Medium (6 + Constitution modifier at 1st level, 1d6 per level thereafter)
- Armor Proficiency: Light armor
- Weapon Proficiency: Simple weapons and symbolic weapons (dagger, staff, relics)
- Tool Proficiency: Divination tools

PROVISIONAL PLAYTEST CONTRACT. Charisma is the Oracle class ability. S = 1/2/3/4 at Oracle levels 1/5/11/17. M = max(1, Charisma modifier). Revelation attack = d20 + normal character proficiency bonus + Charisma modifier. Revelation save DC = 8 + normal character proficiency bonus + Charisma modifier.

HIT DIE CONFLICT. The class overview registry lists d8, while the detailed Player Rulebook Oracle entry states 6 + Constitution modifier at level 1 and d6 per Oracle level thereafter. This pilot uses the detailed rulebook d6 chassis: 6 + Constitution modifier at Oracle 1 and 1d6 per later Oracle level. The d8 registry value remains a preserved alternative pending owner adjudication.

The Oracle is an exceptional progression class rather than a 25-choice Base/Arch/Hero class. Base Oracle is levels 1-2, Revelation Tier development is the Arch-stage progression at levels 3-9, and Revelation Mastery is the Hero-stage progression at levels 10-20. Mystery and Curse are chosen at level 1 but their later Revelations, Boons and Final abilities remain class-level gated.

Choose exactly ONE Mystery and exactly ONE Curse at Oracle level 1. They are not parallel subclasses that stack. You never gain another Mystery or Curse unless a future explicit retraining or transformation rule says otherwise. Curse limitations are permanent and cannot be removed by ordinary cleansing, curse removal, resurrection or rest.

REVELATION CHARGES are the Oracle's only class power resource. Oracles do not use Resonance Points, spell slots, Weave Dissonance, Overcast or ordinary spell-rank casting for Revelations. Revelation Charges are abbreviated RC. A Revelation card's printed RC cost is paid once before resolution. An illegal activation spends nothing.

RC CAP SCHEDULE for this provisional module: maximum 3 at Oracle levels 1-2, 4 at levels 3-5, 5 at levels 6-8, 6 at levels 9-10 and 7 at level 11+. Increasing the maximum does not automatically refill spent charges except where another feature explicitly says so.

RC RECOVERY. A completed long rest restores RC to maximum. A completed short rest restores 1 spent RC, never above maximum. Each Mystery has exactly one Prophecy Trigger. During a real encounter, your Prophecy Trigger can restore at most 1 RC per round and never above maximum. Trivial enemies, willing staged targets, summoned sacrifices, repeated corpse abuse, copied damage, copied healing and events generated solely to trigger recovery do not qualify.

A single event can satisfy only one Prophecy Trigger recovery for you even if several descriptions could apply. A Revelation repeated or copied by another feature does not independently trigger Prophecy recovery unless that feature explicitly says it counts as a new Revelation use. Restored charges never recursively trigger another recovery.

At Oracle level 11, the preserved registry draft additionally sets your starting RC when a real encounter begins to at least max(3, your Charisma modifier), capped by your current RC maximum. If you already have more RC, keep the higher current value. This encounter-start floor occurs once per encounter and cannot be triggered by staged initiative.

REVELATIONS are divine class techniques, not ordinary spells. Unless a Revelation explicitly says otherwise, magical silence does not prevent a purely mental Revelation, but a Revelation requiring speech, touch, visible symbolism or another listed component obeys that requirement. Revelations do not generate Dissonance and cannot be modified by spell-slot, RP, spell-rank discount or ordinary spell-proc mechanics.

One concentration effect may be maintained at a time unless a specific Oracle feature explicitly creates a narrow exception. Damage uses the current Core Systems concentration procedure; if half-damage rounding is required in this pilot, round down. Sustained Revelation control effects grant repeat saves at the cadence printed on the Revelation.

HARD CONTROL GUARD. A creature can suffer at most one Oracle-caused full-turn denial such as Stunned, Paralyzed, Incapacitated by control, or loss of its full Action from your Oracle features per encounter. After that succeeds once, later Oracle effects from you that would fully deny its turn instead remove its Reaction and reduce its Speed to 0 until the end of its next turn after the required save fails.

ADDITIONAL ACTION GUARD. All sources share a maximum of one additional full Action per creature per round. An additional Action cannot grant another additional Action, Bonus Action or Reaction. Features that say a creature acts out of turn use this same cap. Movement-only grants do not count as additional full Actions.

Healing from Oracle features cannot exceed maximum HP, resurrect the dead or remove Wounds unless a feature explicitly says so. Temporary HP never stack. Encounter-only regeneration and drain cannot be farmed outside meaningful combat. Corpse effects create no XP, loot, RC or repeatable corpse resources unless expressly stated.

Copied, echoed, repeated or redirected damage/healing uses only the explicitly copied numeric component. It cannot repeat RC recovery, Prophecy Triggers, action grants, conditions, passive triggers, resource restoration or another copy effect unless the copying feature explicitly says otherwise.

BASE PROGRESSION. At Oracle 1 choose Mystery and Curse; gain the RC engine, Channel Revelation, Prophetic Insight and Divine Sense. At Oracle 2 gain Curse Manifestation, Oracular Trance and Oracle's Burden. No Base feature-selection currency or Skill Points are used.

ARCH-STAGE PROGRESSION. Oracle 3: choose one Tier I Revelation from your Mystery and automatically gain Curse Boon I. Oracle 4: separate ASI or eligible feat. Oracle 5: automatically gain Curse Boon II; any Mystery-specific level-5 feature recorded by the registry belongs to the Mystery module below and does not create an additional generic feature here. Oracle 6: choose one Tier II Revelation and gain Mystery Surge. Oracle 8: separate ASI or eligible feat. Oracle 9: gain your Mystery's fixed Tier III Revelation.

HERO-STAGE PROGRESSION. Oracle 10: gain Curse Boon III and Grand Revelation Foreshadow. Oracle 11: RC cap reaches 7 and the preserved encounter-start RC floor becomes active. Oracle 12: gain the Mystery-specific Mystic Insight. Oracle 13: preserved registry draft grants a second Tier II Revelation; after each completed short rest choose which of your two Tier II Revelations is Attuned for features that specifically refer to an active Tier II option, though both remain known and usable. Oracle 14: separate ASI or eligible feat. Oracle 15: gain Revelation Mastery and Curse Boon IV. Oracle 16: preserved Prophecy Trigger Mastery. Oracle 17: preserved Grand Revelation Foreshadow upgrade. Oracle 18: Prophetic Surge. Oracle 19: separate ASI or eligible feat. Oracle 20: gain the Mystery's Final Revelation and Curse Boon V.

REVELATION MASTERY at level 15 never grants a Revelation you do not know. Choose one mastery enhancement explicitly listed by your Mystery for a Revelation you know. If a listed mastery names a Revelation you did not select, that mastery option is unavailable. Mastery cannot reduce RC below 0, create additional Actions beyond the shared cap or remove repeat-save requirements unless its exact Mystery text says so.

PROPHECY AND DIVINATION never force player beliefs, future choices or confessions. Information features can provide truthful clues, likely outcomes, symbolic visions or current facts, but the Director is not required to reveal unknowable future player decisions. Effects that command or compel a player-controlled character preserve the player's choice of targets and prohibit self-harm, permanent allegiance changes and belief alteration.

CURSE MANIFESTATION provides a common operational intensification rather than ten different hidden sub-engines. When activated, your Curse becomes visibly or metaphysically dominant for 3 rounds: gain +1 on Revelation attacks and +1 to Revelation save DC, but take -1 AC and -1 on your own saving throws for the duration. Any Curse limitation that can normally be voluntarily suppressed cannot be suppressed during Manifestation.

CURSE BOONS are automatic at Oracle levels 3, 5, 10, 15 and 20 for your ONE selected Curse. You do not choose between Curse Boons and do not gain Boons from unselected Curses. Each Curse card below operationalizes all five Boon gates and its permanent limitations.

All class gates use Oracle class level, not total character level. Normal proficiency bonus follows the game's ordinary total-character-level rule. Non-Oracle levels do not raise RC maximum, unlock Curse Boons, Revelations, Mystic Insight, Revelation Mastery, Prophetic Surge or Final Revelation.

INTEGRATION PRECEDENCE. The source ceiling draft conflicts with the registry on short rests and extra Revelation activations. This pilot explicitly chooses 1 RC on a completed short rest and permits a second Revelation only through an expressly granted extra Action or double-cast feature, never more than two Revelation resolutions per round. The preserved ceiling alternative is no short-rest recovery and one activation per round; do not combine both configurations.

All combat-triggered RC refunds, including Prophecy, damage riders and Curse Boons, share ONE recovered RC per round. Rest recovery, encounter-start floor and explicitly limited Final refills are separate. A free, copied or refunded use cannot recursively trigger another recovery. Apply this global cap even if an individual card says its recovery is separate.

The shared Prophetic Surge and your Mystery-specific level-18 Prophecy are two descriptions/options of ONE long-rest use, not two charges. Framework cards such as Tier III, Mystic Insight, Mastery and Final Revelation unlock the named Mystery card and never grant duplicate uses. A level-5 registry Mystery label grants no extra unnamed ability.

Oracle zone default: choose at most three allies and three enemies for each pulse or aura benefit, fixed at activation unless the card names fewer. Persistent damaging/healing zones require concentration and at most three pulses. Pay 1 RC before each pulse after the first, or end the effect; once-per-long-rest Finals include their printed pulses in that limited use. One pulse can affect a creature only once per round. Passive personal resistance and a single-target temporary buff are not zones.

Revelation backlash is the local one-turn penalty of -1 to attacks and saves and -5 feet Speed, ending at the end of your next turn. It is not a level of the general Exhaustion condition. Wound Eater's missed-feeding Exhaustion remains the general condition.

Initiative adjustments change order beginning next round. They never produce or erase a regular turn in the current round; a creature still gets at most one regular turn each round. Ties use the original initiative order. Fixed encounter-start bonuses do not retroactively reorder an encounter.

Extra attacks and echoed damage use base weapon/spell dice before critical-hit extra dice. A redirected damage event cannot be reduced a second time, reflected again or generate RC. Charmed alone does not grant command of a creature's actions.

Oracle’s exceptional ASI/feat schedule is 4, 8, 14 and 19. It does not gain extra ASIs at 12 or 16 from the generic class schedule. The rejected five-milestone draft remains in superseded Playtest Overrides for traceability.

**Protected source interface:** Oracle does not track Weave Dissonance. Revelation Charges, the permanent Curse, and Prophecy Triggers are the Oracle's anti-spam architecture.

## Advancement and resources

RP authority: No RP: protected class contract. Rank access is separate from RP capacity. ASI/feat milestones follow general advancement and are not duplicated by a card.

| Class level | Stage | RP maximum | Max ordinary spell rank | Class choices / automatic grants |
|---:|---|---:|---:|---|
| 1 | Base | 0 | — | Choose ONE Mystery and ONE Curse; Mystery identity/Prophecy, RC 3, Channel Revelation, Insight, Sense |
| 2 | Base | 0 | — | Curse Manifestation, Trance and Burden |
| 3 | Arch | 0 | — | Choose ONE of your Mystery’s three Tier I options; Curse Boon I; RC 4 |
| 4 | Arch | 0 | — | General advancement only; ASI or feat |
| 5 | Arch | 0 | — | Curse Boon II |
| 6 | Arch | 0 | — | Choose ONE of three Tier II options; Mystery Surge; RC 5 |
| 7 | Arch | 0 | — | General advancement only |
| 8 | Arch | 0 | — | General advancement only; ASI or feat |
| 9 | Arch | 0 | — | Fixed Mystery Tier III; RC 6 |
| 10 | Hero | 0 | — | Curse Boon III; Final foreshadow only |
| 11 | Hero | 0 | — | RC 7 and encounter-start floor |
| 12 | Hero | 0 | — | Chosen Mystery Mystic Insight |
| 13 | Hero | 0 | — | Choose a SECOND, distinct Tier II option |
| 14 | Hero | 0 | — | Oracle-specific advancement milestone; ASI or feat |
| 15 | Hero | 0 | — | Choose one eligible Mystery mastery; Curse Boon IV |
| 16 | Hero | 0 | — | Prophecy Trigger Mastery |
| 17 | Hero | 0 | — | Foreshadow — Proven Outcome |
| 18 | Hero | 0 | — | ONE Prophetic Surge use shared with Mystery Prophecy variant |
| 19 | Hero | 0 | — | General advancement only; ASI or feat |
| 20 | Hero | 0 | — | Chosen Mystery Final Revelation; selected Curse Boon V |

Source-listed options for mutually exclusive paths are alternatives, not simultaneous grants. A minimum level on an Arch card may be above9; that card remains an Arch option during later progression.

See [legal build examples](../CLASS_PLAYTEST_BUILD_EXAMPLES.md) for actual level-by-level choices and exceptional progression combinations.

## Worked encounter

1. Oracle 5 example: Charisma 16 gives M=3, PB=3 and S=2. The Oracle has maximum RC 4 under the pilot schedule, begins a real encounter at RC 4, selected Spiral-Touched as the Curse, and knows one Tier I Revelation costing 1 RC.
2. Turn 1: use the known Tier I Revelation for 1 RC, so RC 4→3. Suppose its Mystery Prophecy Trigger does not occur. Because Revelations use RC only, no RP, spell slot, Burn or Dissonance value changes.
3. Turn 2: use Curse Manifestation as a Bonus Action for 1 RC, RC 3→2. For 3 rounds the Oracle gains +1 to Revelation attacks and save DC but suffers -1 AC and -1 on its own saving throws. The permanent Spiral-Touched limitations continue.
4. Enemy turn: the Oracle fails a saving throw. Spiral-Touched Curse Boon I is already unlocked at Oracle 3, so once per long rest the Oracle rerolls that failed save and uses the new result. This reroll costs no RC.
5. Turn 3: Spiral-Touched Curse Boon II is unlocked at Oracle 5. Spend 1 RC to take its once-per-combat out-of-turn Action after another creature finishes its turn, RC 2→1. This uses the universal one-additional-full-Action-per-round allowance and cannot generate another extra Action.
6. After combat, a completed short rest restores 1 RC, RC 1→2. A long rest would instead restore the Oracle to the current maximum of 4. Neither rest removes the Spiral-Touched Curse.

#### Revelation Charges

**Level 1 · automatic · oracle.supplement.shared.revelation-charges**

| Activation | Cost | Range / targets | Duration | Frequency |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Passive | 0 | Self | Permanent | Automatic at Oracle 1 |

Gain the Revelation Charge resource. Maximum RC follows the pilot schedule: 3 at Oracle 1-2, 4 at 3-5, 5 at 6-8, 6 at 9-10 and 7 at 11+. Long rest restores all RC; short rest restores 1. Mystery Prophecy Triggers restore at most 1 RC per round in meaningful encounters.

Prerequisite: Oracle 1

#### Channel Revelation

**Level 1 · automatic · oracle.supplement.shared.channel-revelation**

| Activation | Cost | Range / targets | Duration | Frequency |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Passive | Use the Revelation's printed RC cost | As the selected Revelation | As the selected Revelation | Whenever a known Revelation is legally usable |

Channel Revelation is the framework for activating a known Mystery Revelation. Use that Revelation's printed activation, target, range, RC cost, duration, recharge and save/attack procedure. Channel Revelation adds no extra Action or RC cost and cannot be activated without a known Revelation.

#### Divine Sense

**Level 1 · supplement · oracle.supplement.shared.divine-sense**

| Activation | Cost | Range / targets | Duration | Frequency |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Action | 0 RC | Self; 60-foot sense | 1 minute | 1/short rest |

For 1 minute sense the presence, approximate direction and broad nature of divine, undead and extraplanar creatures or active presences within 60 feet that are not behind an effect specifically blocking divination. You learn only broad categories such as celestial/divine, undead, fiendish, fey, elemental or other extraplanar, not identity, alignment, statistics or intentions.

### Curse

#### Spiral-Touched

**Level 1 · choose one Curse at1 · oracle.supplement.curse.spiral-touched**

| Activation | Cost | Range / targets | Duration | Frequency |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Passive | 0 | Self | Permanent | Chosen once at Oracle 1 |

LIMITATIONS: your temporal dislocation is obvious in conversation. Once per real encounter, at the start of each of your regular turns until this occurs, roll d20; on 1 you lose your Bonus Action and Reaction for that turn as you desynchronize, but retain Action and movement. You have disadvantage on ability checks requiring more than 10 uninterrupted minutes of mundane concentration.

Prerequisite: Selected Curse: Spiral-Touched

#### Wound Eater

**Level 1 · choose one Curse at1 · oracle.supplement.curse.wound-eater**

| Activation | Cost | Range / targets | Duration | Frequency |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Passive | 0 | Self | Permanent | Chosen once at Oracle 1 |

LIMITATIONS: HP recovery from rests and from another creature's healing is halved, rounded down; your own Wound Eater drains and your own Revelations are not halved. Before a long rest completes, you must have successfully drained HP from a meaningful hostile living creature since your previous long rest or gain one level of Exhaustion after the rest. Your corpse-like appearance imposes disadvantage on the first friendly social check with an unfamiliar mortal who can clearly see you, unless circumstances make the appearance irrelevant.

Prerequisite: Selected Curse: Wound Eater

#### Soul-Cracked

**Level 1 · choose one Curse at1 · oracle.supplement.curse.soul-cracked**

| Activation | Cost | Range / targets | Duration | Frequency |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Passive | 0 | Self | Permanent | Chosen once at Oracle 1 |

LIMITATIONS: divination that attempts to read your alignment, aura or intent returns inconsistent results unless it specifically overcomes Oracle curses. Once each encounter, before your first damaging Revelation resolves, roll d6: 1 fire, 2 cold, 3 lightning, 4 necrotic, 5 psychic, 6 radiant; that Revelation's damage becomes the rolled type. While subject to a geas, binding oath or magically enforced pact, the first time each turn you knowingly act against its terms you take S psychic damage.

Prerequisite: Selected Curse: Soul-Cracked

          "description": "After rolling initiative, choose once per combat to shift the damage type of your next Revelation (fire, cold, lightning, necrotic, psychic, or radiant). Until that Revelation resolves, your aura reads as blank to divination — scrying your intent fails for the round."
        },
        {
          "level": 10,
          "name": "Curse Boon III",
          "description": "When you would be charmed or dominated, reflect the effect onto the source (once per long rest). The source must save or suffer the effect instead."
        },
        {
          "level": 15,
          "name": "Curse Boon IV",
          "description": "Fractured soul: gain resistance to psychic damage and +2 to saves vs control. Divination targeting you has 50% chance to return contradictory results."
        },
        {
          "level": 20,
          "name": "Curse Boon V",
          "description": "Once per day, shatter a binding curse, geas, or possession on yourself or an ally within 3 tiles. The broken magic lashes the originator for psychic damage."
        }
      ],

    }

]

#### Fate-Bound

**Level 1 · choose one Curse at1 · oracle.supplement.curse.fate-bound**

| Activation | Cost | Range / targets | Duration | Frequency |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Passive | 0 | Self | Permanent | Chosen once at Oracle 1 |

LIMITATIONS: when you voluntarily swear a specific oath or explicitly accept a prophetic obligation in play, record it. Knowingly and voluntarily breaking it costs 1 RC if available and deals S d6 psychic damage; if you have 0 RC you still take the damage. This never forces you to keep the oath. When the Director presents a clearly identified foreseen disaster that you previously accepted as an omen, deliberately abandoning it without confronting its immediate consequence gives -2 on saving throws until you confront the omen, the omen passes, or you complete a long rest; this is consequence, not compelled behavior.

Uncontrolled undead within 3 tiles that have no specific reason to attack you begin neutral rather than automatically hostile, and you reduce the first necrotic damage you take each round by PB. Once per long rest as an Action animate one Small or Medium corpse within 2 tiles as a 1-HP skeletal attendant for up to 3 rounds. It cannot attack. ### Cataclysm

#### Cataclysm Prophecy Trigger

**Level 1 · automatic for chosen Mystery · oracle.supplement.cataclysm.cataclysm-prophecy-trigger**

| Activation | Cost | Range / targets | Duration | Frequency |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Triggered | 0 RC | Self | Instantaneous | At most once per round during a real encounter |

When one of your Revelations deals damage to at least two meaningful hostile creatures as part of the same area effect, regain 1 RC, never above maximum. Repeated pulses of the same Revelation can trigger this at most once per round.

**Level 1 · automatic for chosen Mystery · oracle.supplement.cataclysm.tremor-sense**

|---|---|---|---|---|
| Passive and Action | 0 RC | Self; enemies within 2 tiles for tremor | Permanent; tremor penalties until end of your next turn | Passive always; tremor 1/short rest |

While touching the ground or a structure, you can identify obvious structural instability, hollow spaces and hidden passages within 3 tiles that transmit vibration, subject to ordinary concealment and construction. Reduce the first bludgeoning damage you take each round by 1. #### Spiral Echo

**Level 1 · automatic for chosen Mystery · oracle.supplement.time.spiral-echo**

| Activation | Cost | Range / targets | Duration | Frequency |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Passive and Reaction | 0 RC | Self; one incoming attack, spell or condition effect | Passive permanent; delayed effect until start of your next regular turn | Delay 1/short rest |

Gain +2 initiative. Once initiative order is established, hostile effects cannot move your initiative slot later unless they explicitly overcome temporal immunity. Once per short rest when an attack, spell or condition effect would resolve solely against you, use your Reaction to delay its resolution until the start of your next regular turn. Record the original attack roll, save DC, damage roll and effect exactly; no resources are refunded and the source need not remain present. At the start of your next turn, before actions, resolve the recorded effect against you using the recorded values, then you may move 1 tile without provoking. Delaying creates no copy or additional proc.

Prerequisite: Oracle 1; Mystery: Time

<details><summary>Preserved supplemental source wording</summary>

      "description": "Passive: you gain +2 initiative and cannot have your place in initiative order reduced below your current slot. Once per short rest, delay one spell, attack, or condition effect targeting you by 1 round — it resolves at the start of your next turn instead, and you may move 1 tile without provoking when it lands."
    }
  }
]
```

</details>

+3 initiative; 1/long rest reroll one failed save and use the new result.

[
  {
    "reference": "CURSES.ORACLE_CURSES[0]",
    "value": {
      "id": "spiral-touched",
      "name": "Spiral-Touched",
      "blurb": "Manipulates initiative, gains time-based echoes",
      "limitations": [
        "You sometimes respond to events before they happen, causing confusion in social situations",
        "You occasionally experience time differently than others, potentially missing turns in combat (5% chance)",
        "You cannot maintain concentration on mundane tasks for extended periods"
      ],
      "boons": [

          "level": 3,
          "name": "Curse Boon I",

          "level": 10,

    }

        "Before each long rest ends, you must drain life from a living foe or gain Exhaustion",
        "Your flesh carries a hollow, withered look that makes many mortals recoil at first meeting"
      ],
      "boons": [
        {
          "level": 3,
          "name": "Curse Boon I",
          "description": "When you hit a living foe with a weapon or touch attack, drain 1d6 HP; you heal for half the amount drained. Undead and constructs are immune."
        },
        {
          "level": 5,
          "name": "Curse Boon II",
          "description": "When you reduce a living foe to 0 HP, regain 1 Revelation Charge. Resistance to necrotic damage from non-Revelation sources."
        },
        {
          "level": 10,
          "name": "Curse Boon III",
          "description": "Once per short rest, siphon a dying enemy's remaining HP as a burst heal (up to 25% of your max HP). The Pale Ledger records their final breath."
        },
        {
          "level": 15,
          "name": "Curse Boon IV",
          "description": "Drain aura: at the start of your turn, living enemies within 2 tiles take necrotic damage; you heal for the total drained. You may suppress this aura in civilized areas."
        },
        {
          "level": 20,
          "name": "Curse Boon V",
          "description": "Once per day, become a conduit of the unfinished hunger — all living enemies within 4 tiles lose 25% max HP; you heal for half the total. Allies are unaffected."
        }
      ],
      "draft": true,
      "source": "Draft — Aethel lore, pending author review"
    }
  }
]
```

</details>

### Soul-Cracked

#### Soul-Cracked — Boon I

**Level 3 · automatic for selected Curse · oracle.supplement.soul-cracked.soul-cracked-boon-i**

| Activation | Cost | Range / targets | Duration | Frequency |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Passive; triggered reroll | 0 | Self | Permanent immunity; immediate reroll | Reroll 1/long rest |

immune to Charmed effects whose purpose is compulsion or domination; 1/long rest reroll one failed save against another mind-affecting effect.

Prerequisite: Oracle 3; selected Curse:Soul-Cracked

<details><summary>Preserved supplemental source wording</summary>

```json
[
  {
    "reference": "CURSES.ORACLE_CURSES[2]",
    "value": {
      "id": "soul-cracked",
      "name": "Soul-Cracked",
      "blurb": "A fractured spirit refuses easy command and answers magic unevenly",
      "limitations": [
        "Divination reads your aura and alignment inconsistently; even allies struggle to scry your intent",
        "Once each encounter, the resonance type of your Revelations may shift without warning",
        "Binding oaths and geas magic cause you physical pain until they are resolved"
      ],
      "boons": [
        {
          "level": 3,
          "name": "Curse Boon I",
          "description": "Immune to charm and compulsion. Once per day, reroll a failed save against mind-affecting effects as your soul fractures around the blow."
        },
        {
          "level": 5,
          "name": "Curse Boon II",
          "description": "After rolling initiative, choose once per combat to shift the damage type of your next Revelation (fire, cold, lightning, necrotic, psychic, or radiant). Until that Revelation resolves, your aura reads as blank to divination — scrying your intent fails for the round."
        },
        {
          "level": 10,
          "name": "Curse Boon III",
          "description": "When you would be charmed or dominated, reflect the effect onto the source (once per long rest). The source must save or suffer the effect instead."
        },
        {
          "level": 15,
          "name": "Curse Boon IV",
          "description": "Fractured soul: gain resistance to psychic damage and +2 to saves vs control. Divination targeting you has 50% chance to return contradictory results."
        },
        {
          "level": 20,
          "name": "Curse Boon V",
          "description": "Once per day, shatter a binding curse, geas, or possession on yourself or an ally within 3 tiles. The broken magic lashes the originator for psychic damage."
        }
      ],
      "draft": true,
      "source": "Draft — Aethel lore, pending author review"
    }
  }
]
```

</details>

+1 AC against the first attack made against you each round; 1/long rest when initiative begins, declare the spirits' warning and you cannot be Surprised in that encounter.

[
  {
    "reference": "CURSES.ORACLE_CURSES[3]",
    "value": {
      "id": "haunted",
      "name": "Haunted",
      "blurb": "Restless dead attend your steps, offering aid at a social cost",
      "limitations": [
        "Restless spirits draw attention: you suffer −2 to Stealth in haunted places or crowded civilized areas",
        "Spirits sometimes speak through you at inopportune moments; social checks may require a Wisdom save",
        "You sometimes address people who are not yet present, unsettling those who witness it"
      ],
      "boons": [
        {
          "level": 3,
          "name": "Curse Boon I",
          "description": "Spectral attendants grant +1 AC against the first attack each round. Once per day, a spirit whispers a warning — you cannot be surprised."
        },
        {
          "level": 5,
          "name": "Curse Boon II",
          "description": "Once per combat, a bound spirit intercepts a single-target attack against you, absorbing up to 5 + level damage before dissipating. The spirit wails as it fades — the attacker must save or suffer −2 to their next attack as dread clings to them."
        },
        {
          "level": 10,
          "name": "Curse Boon III",
          "description": "Spirits murmur of danger — you and allies within 3 tiles cannot be surprised while you are conscious. Echoes of the dead grant +2 to Insight checks nearby."
        },
        {
          "level": 15,
          "name": "Curse Boon IV",
          "description": "Summon a wailing chorus: enemies within 3 tiles suffer Dread (−10% hit chance and saves) for 2 turns. Once per long rest."
        },
        {
          "level": 20,
          "name": "Curse Boon V",
          "description": "Once per day, bind a hostile or friendly spirit to scout, speak with the dead at a distance, or deliver a prophetic message along a path you have walked. The spirit persists for 10 minutes or until it completes one task; when it returns, it whispers what it saw and one name you did not send it to find."
        }
      ],
      "draft": true,
      "source": "Draft — Aethel lore, pending author review"
    }
  }
]

### Tongues

#### Tongues — Boon I

**Level 3 · automatic for selected Curse · oracle.supplement.tongues.tongues-boon-i**

| Activation | Cost | Range / targets | Duration | Frequency |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Passive while chorus unsuppressed | 0 | Self and up to three hearing allies within 25 feet | While interpreting | Continuous |

understand all spoken mortal languages and fragmentary divine script; allies within 25 feet who can hear your interpretation gain +2 on Lore and Insight checks directly involving that communication.

Prerequisite: Oracle 3; selected Curse:Tongues

<details><summary>Preserved supplemental source wording</summary>

```json
[
  {
    "reference": "CURSES.ORACLE_CURSES[4]",
    "value": {
      "id": "tongues",
      "name": "Tongues",
      "blurb": "Many voices share your speech, making truth harder to hear cleanly",
      "limitations": [
        "Unless you suppress the curse, social checks with mortals who do not share your mystery tongue suffer −2",
        "Even accurate prophetic speech is often misheard; listeners may hear what they fear",
        "You cannot fully silence the chorus; concentrating on mundane tasks longer than 1 hour requires a Wisdom save"
      ],
      "boons": [
        {
          "level": 3,
          "name": "Curse Boon I",
          "description": "Understand all spoken languages and read divine script fragments. Allies within 5 tiles gain +2 to Lore and Insight checks when you interpret for them."
        },

          "level": 5,
          "name": "Curse Boon II",
          "description": "Confounding babble: one enemy within 3 tiles must save or lose their next action, overwhelmed by overlapping voices. Once per combat."
        },
        {
          "level": 10,
          "name": "Curse Boon III",
          "description": "Prophetic utterance — force one creature within 3 tiles to truthfully answer a yes/no question drawn from the Choir's echo. Once per day."
        },
        {
          "level": 15,
          "name": "Curse Boon IV",
          "description": "Polyglot surge: communicate with beasts, plants, and spirits for 1 hour as the First Song's vocabulary flows through you. Once per long rest."
        },
        {
          "level": 20,
          "name": "Curse Boon V",
          "description": "Once per day, speak a single Word in the tongue of creation — all who hear must save or obey one short command. Those who resist take psychic damage."
        }
      ],
      "draft": true,
      "source": "Draft — Aethel lore, pending author review"
    }
  }
]

gain fire resistance. Once per round when a melee weapon attack deals damage to you, the attacker takes 1d4 fire damage. The first time each round your curse blood ignites, hostile creatures within 5 feet take 1 additional fire damage.

Prerequisite: Oracle 3; selected Curse:Burning Blood

  {

      "limitations": [
        "When slashing or piercing damage wounds you, you and adjacent foes take 1d4 fire damage as your blood sparks",

          "level": 15,

**Level 3 · automatic for selected Curse · oracle.supplement.false-life.false-life-boon-i**

| Activation | Cost | Range / targets | Duration | Frequency |
|---|---|---|---|---|

advantage on saves against death effects and hostile necrotic/negative-energy effects. 1/long rest when you would die from failed death saves or be reduced to 0 by an effect that does not kill outright, remain at 1 HP instead.

Prerequisite: Oracle 3; selected Curse:False Life

<details><summary>Preserved supplemental source wording</summary>

[
  {
    "reference": "CURSES.ORACLE_CURSES[6]",
    "value": {

      "limitations": [
        "Healing spells cast on you without a successful Insight check have a 50% chance to fail; magic reads you as corpse-cold",
        "Unless you suppress the curse, divination treats you as undead",
        "Clerics of life-deities may hesitate to aid you without proof that you still breathe"
      ],
      "boons": [
        {
          "level": 3,

        },
        {
          "level": 5,
          "name": "Curse Boon II",
          "description": "Feign death perfectly for 1 minute — undead ignore you unless attacked; you remain conscious but motionless. Once per combat."
        },
        {
          "level": 10,
          "name": "Curse Boon III",
          "description": "When reduced to 0 HP, remain conscious for 1 round before falling — you may move, speak, or cast one Revelation. Once per long rest."
        },
        {

### Shattered Mind

#### Shattered Mind — Boon I

**Level 3 · automatic for selected Curse · oracle.supplement.shattered-mind.shattered-mind-boon-i**

| Activation | Cost | Range / targets | Duration | Frequency |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Bonus Action | 0 for movement; normal RC for Revelation | Self or normal Revelation targets | Immediate; Revelation option forfeits full Action | 1/encounter |

once per encounter choose one: move up to your Speed as a Bonus Action without provoking opportunity attacks; or activate one known Tier I Revelation using a Bonus Action instead of its normal Action. If you use the Revelation option, you cannot also take a full Action that turn.

Prerequisite: Oracle 3; selected Curse:Shattered Mind

#### Consumed — Boon I

**Level 3 · automatic for selected Curse · oracle.supplement.consumed.consumed-boon-i**

| Activation | Cost | Range / targets | Duration | Frequency |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Passive; Action to suppress appearance | 0 | Self | Damage reduction permanent; suppression one hour | Reduction first theme damage/round; suppression 1/long rest |

reduce the first instance of your Mystery theme damage you take each round by PB. Once per long rest suppress visible transformation for 1 hour, though divination still detects your Mystery.

Prerequisite: Oracle 3; selected Curse:Consumed

  {
    "reference": "CURSES.ORACLE_CURSES[8]",

      "limitations": [
        "As you gain levels, your appearance shifts toward your Mystery's theme: flame hair, starlit eyes, bone protrusions, and the like",
        "Some civilized NPCs react with fear or awe; concealing the change in formal courts requires ongoing effort",
        "As your body changes, armor and gear must be refitted periodically (roleplay / downtime cost)"
      ],

        {
          "level": 3,
          "name": "Curse Boon I",

Allies within 2 tiles gain +1 AC as your transformation steadies the battlefield; suppression is impossible while the embodiment lasts."
        }
      ],

    }
  }
]

### Fate-Bound

#### Fate-Bound — Boon I

**Level 3 · automatic for selected Curse · oracle.supplement.fate-bound.fate-bound-boon-i**

| Activation | Cost | Range / targets | Duration | Frequency |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Triggered before a declared check/save | 0 | One ally within 30 feet | One named roll before scene ends | 1/long rest |

1/long rest before an ally within 30 feet makes a check or save tied to a declared party decision, speak an omen and give +2 to that roll. If the party changes plans before the roll, the named ally retains the bonus for the originally described type of challenge until the end of the scene.

Prerequisite: Oracle 3; selected Curse:Fate-Bound

<details><summary>Preserved supplemental source wording</summary>

```json
[
  {
    "reference": "CURSES.ORACLE_CURSES[9]",
    "value": {
      "id": "fate-bound",
      "name": "Fate-Bound",
      "blurb": "Foreknowledge marks your path, but each omen narrows the road ahead",
      "limitations": [
        "You cannot voluntarily break a sworn oath or prophecy without losing 1 Revelation Charge and suffering psychic backlash",
        "Major life choices may feel predetermined once you have seen the threads that lead to them",
        "Trying to flee a foreseen disaster without confronting it imposes −2 to saves until you face the omen"
      ],
      "boons": [
        {
          "level": 3,
          "name": "Curse Boon I",
          "description": "Once per day, glimpse one likely outcome before a party decision — the ally acting gains +2 to the related check or save. You must speak the omen aloud; allies who heard it retain the bonus even if the party chooses a different path."
        },
        {
          "level": 5,
          "name": "Curse Boon II",
          "description": "Fated dodge: once per combat, force an attack against you to reroll as fate rewinds the strike. You must declare after the roll but before damage."
        },
        {
          "level": 10,
          "name": "Curse Boon III",
          "description": "When an ally within 5 tiles would critically fail a save, you may accept the failure on yourself instead (once per long rest). The thread binds to you."
        },
        {
          "level": 15,
          "name": "Curse Boon IV",
          "description": "Thread of destiny: +2 to saves vs death, fate-altering magic, and effects that would remove you from a prophesied path. Once per long rest, reroll a failed death save as the Concord refuses to cut your thread early."
        },
        {
          "level": 20,
          "name": "Curse Boon V",
          "description": "Once per day, declare a prophetic outcome — one ally within 6 tiles auto-succeeds their next save or attack as the Concord enforces the written thread. You must describe the outcome aloud before the roll; if the prophecy fails to come true by end of combat, you lose 1 Revelation Charge as the thread frays."
        }
      ],
      "draft": true,
      "source": "Draft — Aethel lore, pending author review"
    }
  }
]
```

</details>

### Ancestor

#### Echo Voice

**Level 3 · choose one at Oracle 3 · oracle.supplement.ancestor.echo-voice**

| Activation | Cost | Range / targets | Duration | Frequency |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Reaction | 1 RC | One willing ally within 5 tiles; telepathy with willing allies within 5 tiles | Telepathy passive; reroll instantaneous | At will while RC remains; one reroll per failed roll |

You may communicate telepathically with willing allies within 5 tiles. When one such ally fails an attack roll, saving throw or ability check, use your Reaction and spend 1 RC before another roll is made. That ally rerolls the failed roll with +2 and must use the new result. The reroll cannot itself trigger another Echo Voice.

Prerequisite: Oracle 3; Mystery: Ancestor; selected Tier I Revelation

<details><summary>Preserved supplemental source wording</summary>

```json

  {
    "reference": "MYSTERIES.ORACLE_MYSTERIES[0].tierI[0]",
    "value": {
      "name": "Echo Voice",
      "charges": 1,
      "kind": "active",
      "description": "Passive: telepathy with allies within 5 tiles. Active: spend 1 charge to let an ally repeat their last failed roll (attack, save, or skill) with +2."
    }
  }
]
```

</details>

#### Ancestral Strike

**Level 3 · choose one at Oracle 3 · oracle.supplement.ancestor.ancestral-strike**

| Activation | Cost | Range / targets | Duration | Frequency |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Bonus Action | 1 RC | Self; next weapon or touch attack before end of turn | Until end of current turn or until attack resolves | At will while RC remains |

Empower your next weapon or touch attack this turn. On a hit, add damage equal to half the attack's unmodified rolled base damage dice total, rounded down, minimum 1. If that attack is a critical hit, the target makes a Constitution save; on failure it would be Stunned until the end of its next turn, subject to the Oracle hard-control guard.

Prerequisite: Oracle 3; Mystery: Ancestor; selected Tier I Revelation

<details><summary>Preserved supplemental source wording</summary>

    "reference": "MYSTERIES.ORACLE_MYSTERIES[0].tierI[1]",

      "kind": "active",
      "description": "Spend 1 charge to channel a warrior ancestor. Next weapon or touch attack deals +50% bonus weapon damage and stuns on crit."
    }
  }
]
```

</details>

#### Bloodline Sight

**Level 3 · choose one at Oracle 3 · oracle.supplement.ancestor.bloodline-sight**

| Activation | Cost | Range / targets | Duration | Frequency |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Action | 1 RC | Self; 6-tile radius | 2 rounds | At will while RC remains |

For 2 rounds you perceive undead, invisible and ethereal creatures within 6 tiles that are not behind total cover or an effect explicitly blocking supernatural detection. Those creatures cannot gain concealment or hide from you solely through invisibility, ethereal overlap or mundane Stealth while in range. This does not expose illusions unrelated to those categories.

Prerequisite: Oracle 3; Mystery: Ancestor; selected Tier I Revelation

<details><summary>Preserved supplemental source wording</summary>

      "kind": "active",
      "description": "Spend 1 charge to reveal all undead, invisible, or ethereal creatures within 6 tiles for 2 rounds; they cannot benefit from concealment or stealth."
    }
  }
]
```

</details>

#### Spirit Walk

**Level 6 · choose first at Oracle 6; a distinct second at Oracle 13 · oracle.supplement.ancestor.spirit-walk**

| Activation | Cost | Range / targets | Duration | Frequency |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Action | 2 RC | Self | Until end of current turn | At will while RC remains |

Your spirit leaves your body until the end of the turn. During the effect you may move through creatures and nonmagical solid barriers, ignore difficult terrain and cannot be targeted by attacks or effects that require a physical target. You may activate one other known Revelation at its normal RC cost while in spirit form. You cannot manipulate physical objects or make weapon attacks. At turn end choose your original space or a legal unoccupied space you reached; if none is legal, return to the original space. Your physical body remains in its original space and can still be attacked normally; damage and conditions on either form affect your one character. The optional Revelation uses its normal activation and cost: this feature grants no free Action. Ending in an occupied or illegal space returns the spirit to its body.

Prerequisite: Oracle 6; Mystery: Ancestor; selected Tier II Revelation

<details><summary>Preserved supplemental source wording</summary>

```json

  {
    "reference": "MYSTERIES.ORACLE_MYSTERIES[0].tierII[0]",
    "value": {
      "name": "Spirit Walk",
      "charges": 2,
      "kind": "active",
      "description": "Spend 2 charges: leave your body for 1 turn — intangible, move through walls/creatures, cannot be targeted, may cast a Revelation through your spirit at full range."
    }
  }
]
```

</details>

#### Echo Armor

**Level 6 · choose first at Oracle 6; a distinct second at Oracle 13 · oracle.supplement.ancestor.echo-armor**

| Activation | Cost | Range / targets | Duration | Frequency |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Passive | 0 RC | Self | Permanent | Damage reduction always; death prevention 1/short rest |

Reduce the first nonmagical weapon damage you take each round by PB, minimum 0. Once per short rest, when damage would reduce you to 0 HP without killing you outright, remain at 1 HP instead. The prevention removes no Wounds and cannot trigger if another effect has already prevented that same reduction to 0.

Prerequisite: Oracle 6; Mystery: Ancestor; selected Tier II Revelation

    }
  }
]

#### Ghost Chant

**Level 6 · choose first at Oracle 6; a distinct second at Oracle 13 · oracle.supplement.ancestor.ghost-chant**

| Activation | Cost | Range / targets | Duration | Frequency |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Action | 2 RC | Enemies of your choice within 3 tiles | Maximum 2 rounds | At will while RC remains |

Each target makes a Wisdom save. On failure it suffers Dread: -1 on attack rolls and saving throws until the end of its next turn. At the end of that turn it repeats the save, ending Dread on success; failure extends Dread one final round. This is fear-based and a creature immune to fear is immune.

Prerequisite: Oracle 6; Mystery: Ancestor; selected Tier II Revelation

<details><summary>Preserved supplemental source wording</summary>

      "charges": 2,
      "kind": "active",
      "description": "Spend 2 charges to inflict Dread on enemies within 3 tiles (−10% hit chance and saves for 2 turns)."
    }
  }
]
```

</details>

#### Bloodline Convergence

**Level 9 · automatic for chosen Mystery · oracle.supplement.ancestor.bloodline-convergence**

| Activation | Cost | Range / targets | Duration | Frequency |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Action | 3 RC | Self and allies within 5 tiles | 2 rounds; Revelation backlash 1 round | At will while RC remains |

For 2 rounds, once on each of your regular turns you gain one additional full Action usable only to activate a Revelation or Dash/Move, subject to the universal extra-Action cap. Allies within 5 tiles gain +1 on attack rolls, reduce the first damage they take each round by S and are immune to Frightened. Once per round you may activate one known Tier I Ancestor Revelation for 0 RC; it still uses its normal action type. When Convergence ends you are suffering Revelation backlash until the end of your next turn: -1 on attack rolls and saving throws and Speed -5 feet. Concentration is required. At most three chosen allies gain its aura. The free Tier I activation uses its ordinary action type and shares the two-Revelation ceiling.

Prerequisite: Oracle 9; Mystery: Ancestor

<details><summary>Preserved supplemental source wording</summary>

```json
[
  {
    "reference": "MYSTERIES.ORACLE_MYSTERIES[0].tierIII",
    "value": {
      "name": "Bloodline Convergence",
      "charges": 3,
      "kind": "active",

Once during each of your turns while Fury lasts, after your first weapon or touch attack hits, you may make one additional weapon or touch attack as a Bonus Action if you have not already used a Bonus Action that turn; this is not an extra full Action.

#### Ledger's Command

**Level 6 · choose first at Oracle 6; a distinct second at Oracle 13 · oracle.supplement.bones.ledger-s-command**

| Activation | Cost | Range / targets | Duration | Frequency |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Action | 2 RC | Up to 2 undead within 4 tiles | 2 rounds | At will while RC remains |

Each target makes a Wisdom save if hostile or unwilling; willing undead may waive it. On failure you command it for 2 rounds. Once per round as a Bonus Action, command one controlled undead to move up to its Speed and make one ordinary attack, using its own statistics; this obeys the universal one-extra-action limit and all controlled undead share that single Bonus Action command. Controlled undead reduce their first damage each round by 1. Once during the command, one controlled expendable undead may intercept a single-target attack against you and be reduced to 0 HP after absorbing it. This feature creates no corpse, loot or RC farm. Eligible targets are nonlegendary undead of challenge rating no greater than half your Oracle level (round down), with Wisdom saves initially and at each turn end. Commanding uses your shared companion Bonus Action; only one commanded undead makes one attack per round, capped at S d8+M damage with no monster multiattack, recharge or spell resources. Commanding a hostile undead to spend its Action consumes its one hard-denial allowance; later control can only deny Reactions and reduce Speed to 0 on a failed save, never command another Action.

Prerequisite: Oracle 6; Mystery: Bones; selected Tier II Revelation

Each takes force damage equal to half the actual triggering damage rounded down, maximum Oracle level, on failure or half that amount on success; a failing creature also falls Prone. Allies within the burst take no damage and reduce their first damage before the start of your next turn by 1. Reflected damage cannot trigger another reflection, RC refund or recursive damage effect.

Prerequisite: Oracle 6; Mystery: Cataclysm; selected Tier II Revelation

    }
  }
]

Until the zone ends, enemies inside take -1 on saving throws against your Cataclysm Revelations; allies inside reduce first damage each round by 1 and cannot be knocked Prone by this zone. Difficult terrain expands 1 tile outward after the second pulse but deals no extra damage there. Structures may crack at Director discretion. Create one illusory duplicate. Once each round when a creature that can see the duplicate targets you with an attack, it makes a Wisdom save before rolling. On failure it targets the Mirage instead; the attack automatically misses you and destroys the Mirage. On success it targets normally. | Action | 2 RC | One willing living ally within 5 tiles | 2 rounds | At will while RC remains; one Life Link at a time |

Link yourself to the ally. Once per round when it takes damage, you may use your Reaction to transfer up to half that actual damage rounded down to yourself. Transferred damage keeps its type and cannot be redirected again. The ally reduces the first necrotic damage it takes each round by S. Once per round when one of your paid or finite Oracle effects restores HP to the linked ally, it regains an additional 1d4 HP. The link ends if you are more than 5 tiles apart. Apply mitigation to the original damage once before splitting. The transferred portion cannot be mitigated again and cannot generate recovery or retaliation triggers.

Prerequisite: Oracle 6; Mystery: Life; selected Tier II Revelation

]

| Activation | Cost | Range / targets | Duration | Frequency |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Action | 2 RC | 3-tile-radius fixed zone centered within 5 tiles | 2 rounds | At will while RC remains; one Rift at a time |

Enemies beginning their turn in the Rift act after all non-Rift creatures at the same initiative step for that round and take -1 on their first attack roll that turn. Allies beginning their turn there gain +2 for initiative-order comparisons and reduce the first damage from an effect that would remove or delay an Action by S. Once per Rift when an ally inside fails a saving throw, you may spend 1 RC as a Reaction to let it reroll with +2 and use the new result.

Prerequisite: Oracle 6; Mystery: Time; selected Tier II Revelation

<details><summary>Preserved supplemental source wording</summary>

```json
[
  {
    "reference": "MYSTERIES.ORACLE_MYSTERIES[9].tierII[1]",
    "value": {
      "name": "Temporal Rift",
      "charges": 2,
      "kind": "active",
      "description": "Spend 2 charges: open a 3-tile temporal rift for 2 rounds — enemies inside act last in initiative and suffer −10% hit on their first attack each round; allies inside gain +2 initiative and +10% DR vs effects that would reduce their actions; once per rift, when an ally inside fails a save, you may spend 1 charge to let them reroll with +2."
    }
  }
]
```

</details>

#### Paradox Skin

**Level 6 · choose first at Oracle 6; a distinct second at Oracle 13 · oracle.supplement.time.paradox-skin**

| Activation | Cost | Range / targets | Duration | Frequency |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Passive and Reaction | 0 RC | Self; one ally within 3 tiles for time-effect interception | Permanent | Echo avoidance once per round; ally reroll 1/short rest |

Once per round when an attack would hit you, roll d10; on 1 the attack instead hits a temporal echo and misses. You have advantage on saves against magical aging, Slow and explicitly time-based control. Once per short rest when an ally within 3 tiles fails a save against an aging, Slow or explicitly time-based effect, use your Reaction to let it reroll with +2 and use the new result.

Prerequisite: Oracle 6; Mystery: Time; selected Tier II Revelation

<details><summary>Preserved supplemental source wording</summary>

```json
[
  {
    "reference": "MYSTERIES.ORACLE_MYSTERIES[9].tierII[2]",
    "value": {
      "name": "Paradox Skin",
      "kind": "passive",
      "description": "Passive: when hit, 10% chance the attack lands on a temporal echo instead (miss). Resistance to time, aging, and slow effects. Once per short rest, when an ally within 3 tiles fails a save vs a time or aging effect, you may absorb the effect — they reroll with +2 as causality snags around you."
    }
  }
]
```

</details>

#### Spiral Convergence

**Level 9 · automatic for chosen Mystery · oracle.supplement.time.spiral-convergence**

| Activation | Cost | Range / targets | Duration | Frequency |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Action | 3 RC | Self and allies within 5 tiles | 2 rounds; Revelation backlash 1 round | At will while RC remains |

For 2 rounds, once per round when an ally within 5 tiles fails an attack roll, saving throw or ability check, use your Reaction to let that ally reroll and use the new result; this changes only the roll and does not refund resources, Actions, damage or proc triggers from events already resolved. Once on each of your regular turns you gain one additional full Action usable only to activate a Revelation or Dash/Move, subject to the universal extra-Action cap. An ally whose roll you rewind reduces the first damage it takes before your next turn by 1. If an enemy directly benefited from the original failed roll, it makes a Wisdom save; on failure it loses its Reaction until the end of its next turn. After Convergence ends you are suffering Revelation backlash until end of your next turn: -1 attacks/saves and Speed -5 feet.

Prerequisite: Oracle 9; Mystery: Time

<details><summary>Preserved supplemental source wording</summary>

```json
[
  {
    "reference": "MYSTERIES.ORACLE_MYSTERIES[9].tierIII",
    "value": {
      "name": "Spiral Convergence",
      "charges": 3,
      "kind": "active",
      "description": "Spend 3 charges: for 2 turns, once per round rewind one failed ally roll within 5 tiles (attack, save, or skill) as if it never happened — the Turning Spiral folds causality; you gain +1 action per turn (Revelation or move only) and +2 initiative, allies rewound this way gain +10% DR until start of your next turn, and enemies who benefited from the undone roll must save or lose their reaction. After: Exhausted (−1 action, −10% resist for 1 round)."
    }
  }
]
```

</details>

### Spiral-Touched

#### Spiral-Touched — Boon III

**Level 10 · automatic for selected Curse · oracle.supplement.spiral-touched.spiral-touched-boon-iii**

| Activation | Cost | Range / targets | Duration | Frequency |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Triggered at 0 HP | 0 | Self | Until start of next regular turn | 1/long rest |

1/long rest when damage would reduce you to 0 HP without killing you outright, instead regain floor(max HP/4), minimum 1, and phase out until the start of your next regular turn; while phased you cannot be targeted, affect other creatures or activate Revelations.

Prerequisite: Oracle 10; selected Curse:Spiral-Touched

<details><summary>Preserved supplemental source wording</summary>

```json
[
  {
    "reference": "CURSES.ORACLE_CURSES[0]",
    "value": {
      "id": "spiral-touched",
      "name": "Spiral-Touched",
      "blurb": "Manipulates initiative, gains time-based echoes",
      "limitations": [
        "You sometimes respond to events before they happen, causing confusion in social situations",
        "You occasionally experience time differently than others, potentially missing turns in combat (5% chance)",
        "You cannot maintain concentration on mundane tasks for extended periods"
      ],
      "boons": [
        {
          "level": 3,
          "name": "Curse Boon I",
          "description": "See 1 round into the future during initiative (+3 initiative). Once per day, reroll a failed save as you briefly step outside the timestream."
        },
        {
          "level": 5,
          "name": "Curse Boon II",
          "description": "Spend 1 Revelation Charge to act out of turn once per combat. You gain resistance to time-based magic and aging effects."
        },
        {
          "level": 10,
          "name": "Curse Boon III",
          "description": "When you would be reduced to 0 HP, you may instead phase out of time for 1 round, becoming untargetable and regaining 25% of your maximum HP. Once per long rest."
        },
        {
          "level": 15,
          "name": "Curse Boon IV",
          "description": "You exist in multiple timestreams simultaneously, allowing you to make two different actions in a single turn once per combat. Enemies attacking you have a 10% chance to hit your echo instead, causing the attack to miss."
        },
        {
          "level": 20,
          "name": "Curse Boon V",
          "description": "You gain the ability to create a time loop, resetting an entire combat encounter to its beginning state once per day. All allies retain knowledge of the original timeline, allowing them to make different choices."
        }
      ],
      "source": "rulebook — Example Curse: Spiral-Touched (only full curse tree in player rulebook)"
    }
  }
]
```

</details>

#### Spiral-Touched — Boon V

**Level 20 · automatic for selected Curse · oracle.supplement.spiral-touched.spiral-touched-boon-v**

| Activation | Cost | Range / targets | Duration | Frequency |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Reaction before round 4 | 0 | Recorded encounter participants | Restore recorded encounter-start state | 1/long rest |

1/long rest as a Reaction before the fourth round of the encounter begins, rewind the current encounter to its initiative-start snapshot: restore combatant positions, HP, temporary HP, conditions, RC and per-encounter uses to that snapshot. Short-/long-rest uses, physical consumables and this Time Loop use remain spent. Slain encounter participants return to their snapshot state and no duplicate XP, treasure or consumables are created. Allies retain memory of the previous timeline. Time Loop does not restore RP, Ki, Conviction, Blood Gauge, device stock, healing reserves or any other daily/rest resource; only the named RC and per-encounter states rewind. No loot changes ownership through rewinding. Record the snapshot at initiative; if no complete snapshot exists, this option is unavailable and unspent. It cannot undo a previous encounter or duplicate a per-rest use.

Prerequisite: Oracle 20; selected Curse:Spiral-Touched

### Wound Eater

#### Wound Eater — Boon III

**Level 10 · automatic for selected Curse · oracle.supplement.wound-eater.wound-eater-boon-iii**

| Activation | Cost | Range / targets | Duration | Frequency |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Action | 0 | One dying hostile living creature touched | Instantaneous | 1/short rest; no staged/summoned targets |

1/short rest as an Action touch one dying hostile living creature at 0 HP; that creature dies and you regain HP equal to one-quarter your maximum HP rounded down. It cannot trigger from summoned, trivial or staged creatures and removes no Wounds.

Prerequisite: Oracle 10; selected Curse:Wound Eater

          "level": 10,

        },

#### Wound Eater — Boon IV

**Level 15 · automatic for selected Curse · oracle.supplement.wound-eater.wound-eater-boon-iv**

| Activation | Cost | Range / targets | Duration | Frequency |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Bonus Action; start-of-turn pulses | 2 RC start; 1 RC per later pulse | Up to three living enemies within 10 feet | Concentration; at most three pulses | One aura; once/target/round |

pay 2 RC as a Bonus Action to activate a 10-foot drain aura, requiring concentration for at most three pulses; pay 1 RC before each pulse after the first or end it. You may suppress it freely. During a real encounter, at the start of your turn up to three meaningful living enemies in the aura takes S necrotic damage; regain HP equal to the total actually dealt, capped at S+M per turn.

Prerequisite: Oracle 15; selected Curse:Wound Eater

  {

      "blurb": "Lives by wounds taken from the living, never by an easy cure",
      "limitations": [
        "Natural healing and healing granted by another restore only half their usual amount to you",
        "Before each long rest ends, you must drain life from a living foe or gain Exhaustion",
        "Your flesh carries a hollow, withered look that makes many mortals recoil at first meeting"
      ],
      "boons": [
        {
          "level": 3,

        },

        },

        }
      ],

### Soul-Cracked

#### Soul-Cracked — Boon III

**Level 10 · automatic for selected Curse · oracle.supplement.soul-cracked.soul-cracked-boon-iii**

| Activation | Cost | Range / targets | Duration | Frequency |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Triggered when targeted by Charm/domination | 0 | Self and effect source | Until end of source’s next turn | 1/long rest |

1/long rest when another creature would Charm or dominate you, negate that effect against you and force the source to make a Wisdom save against your Revelation DC; on failure it becomes Charmed by you until the end of its next turn, with no belief or permanent allegiance change.

Prerequisite: Oracle 10; selected Curse:Soul-Cracked

<details><summary>Preserved supplemental source wording</summary>

```json
[
  {
    "reference": "CURSES.ORACLE_CURSES[2]",
    "value": {
      "id": "soul-cracked",
      "name": "Soul-Cracked",
      "blurb": "A fractured spirit refuses easy command and answers magic unevenly",
      "limitations": [
        "Divination reads your aura and alignment inconsistently; even allies struggle to scry your intent",
        "Once each encounter, the resonance type of your Revelations may shift without warning",
        "Binding oaths and geas magic cause you physical pain until they are resolved"
      ],
      "boons": [
        {
          "level": 3,
          "name": "Curse Boon I",
          "description": "Immune to charm and compulsion. Once per day, reroll a failed save against mind-affecting effects as your soul fractures around the blow."
        },
        {
          "level": 5,
          "name": "Curse Boon II",
          "description": "After rolling initiative, choose once per combat to shift the damage type of your next Revelation (fire, cold, lightning, necrotic, psychic, or radiant). Until that Revelation resolves, your aura reads as blank to divination — scrying your intent fails for the round."
        },
        {
          "level": 10,
          "name": "Curse Boon III",
          "description": "When you would be charmed or dominated, reflect the effect onto the source (once per long rest). The source must save or suffer the effect instead."
        },
        {
          "level": 15,
          "name": "Curse Boon IV",
          "description": "Fractured soul: gain resistance to psychic damage and +2 to saves vs control. Divination targeting you has 50% chance to return contradictory results."
        },
        {
          "level": 20,
          "name": "Curse Boon V",
          "description": "Once per day, shatter a binding curse, geas, or possession on yourself or an ally within 3 tiles. The broken magic lashes the originator for psychic damage."
        }
      ],
      "draft": true,
      "source": "Draft — Aethel lore, pending author review"
    }
  }
]
```

</details>

### Haunted

#### Haunted — Boon III

**Level 10 · automatic for selected Curse · oracle.supplement.haunted.haunted-boon-iii**

| Activation | Cost | Range / targets | Duration | Frequency |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Passive while conscious | 0 | Self and up to three allies within 15 feet | While in range | Continuous |

while conscious, you and allies within 15 feet cannot be Surprised; such allies gain +2 on Insight checks.

Prerequisite: Oracle 10; selected Curse:Haunted

      "limitations": [
        "Restless spirits draw attention: you suffer −2 to Stealth in haunted places or crowded civilized areas",
        "Spirits sometimes speak through you at inopportune moments; social checks may require a Wisdom save",
        "You sometimes address people who are not yet present, unsettling those who witness it"
      ],

        {

          "name": "Curse Boon I",

          "level": 5,

          "level": 10,

        },
        {

          "name": "Curse Boon IV",
          "description": "Summon a wailing chorus: enemies within 3 tiles suffer Dread (−10% hit chance and saves) for 2 turns. Once per long rest."

        }
      ],

#### Haunted — Boon V

**Level 20 · automatic for selected Curse · oracle.supplement.haunted.haunted-boon-v**

| Activation | Cost | Range / targets | Duration | Frequency |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Action to summon | 0 | One spirit scout; one-mile same-plane route limit | 10 minutes | 1/long rest |

1/long rest summon one spirit scout for 10 minutes. It can travel along a route you have personally traveled on the same plane, up to 1 mile from you, observe with ordinary sight/hearing, speak with willing dead spirits it encounters and deliver one short prophetic message. On return it truthfully reports what it directly observed and may identify one unexpected creature or person's name it actually encountered. It cannot pass sealed planar barriers, read minds or reveal unknowable facts.

Prerequisite: Oracle 20; selected Curse:Haunted

### Tongues

#### Tongues — Boon III

**Level 10 · automatic for selected Curse · oracle.supplement.tongues.tongues-boon-iii**

| Activation | Cost | Range / targets | Duration | Frequency |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Action; speech required | 0 | One creature within 15 feet | One question | 1/long rest |

1/long rest as an Action ask one creature within 15 feet a yes/no question. A non-player creature makes a Charisma save; on failure it must answer truthfully with yes, no or 'I don't know.' A player-controlled creature retains agency and may refuse, in which case the Revelation yields 'unclear' rather than compelling speech.

Prerequisite: Oracle 10; selected Curse:Tongues

        },

          "level": 15,
          "name": "Curse Boon IV",

          "level": 20,
          "name": "Curse Boon V",
          "description": "Once per day, speak a single Word in the tongue of creation — all who hear must save or obey one short command. Those who resist take psychic damage."
        }
      ],
      "draft": true,
      "source": "Draft — Aethel lore, pending author review"
    }
  }
]

#### Tongues — Boon V

**Level 20 · automatic for selected Curse · oracle.supplement.tongues.tongues-boon-v**

| Activation | Cost | Range / targets | Duration | Frequency |

1/long rest as an Action speak one short command of no more than five words to creatures of your choice within 30 feet that can hear you. Each makes a Wisdom save. On failure it follows the command until the end of its next turn, subject to the hard-control guard; the command cannot cause direct self-harm, force belief change, permanent allegiance, confession or surrender of essential possessions. A creature that succeeds takes 3 S d6 psychic damage instead.

          "name": "Curse Boon I",
          "description": "Understand all spoken languages and read divine script fragments. Allies within 5 tiles gain +2 to Lore and Insight checks when you interpret for them."
        },
        {
          "level": 5,
          "name": "Curse Boon II",
          "description": "Confounding babble: one enemy within 3 tiles must save or lose their next action, overwhelmed by overlapping voices. Once per combat."
        },
        {
          "level": 10,
          "name": "Curse Boon III",
          "description": "Prophetic utterance — force one creature within 3 tiles to truthfully answer a yes/no question drawn from the Choir's echo. Once per day."
        },
        {
          "level": 15,
          "name": "Curse Boon IV",
          "description": "Polyglot surge: communicate with beasts, plants, and spirits for 1 hour as the First Song's vocabulary flows through you. Once per long rest."
        },
        {
          "level": 20,
          "name": "Curse Boon V",
          "description": "Once per day, speak a single Word in the tongue of creation — all who hear must save or obey one short command. Those who resist take psychic damage."
        }
      ],
      "draft": true,
      "source": "Draft — Aethel lore, pending author review"
    }
  }
]

### Burning Blood

#### Burning Blood — Boon III

**Level 10 · automatic for selected Curse · oracle.supplement.burning-blood.burning-blood-boon-iii**

| Activation | Cost | Range / targets | Duration | Frequency |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Triggered when blood ignites | 0; optional 1 RC burst | Up to three allies within 10 feet; three enemies within 15 feet | Bonus through start of next turn | Once/round |

when your blood ignites, allies within 10 feet gain +1 damage on their next weapon hit before the start of your next turn. You may spend 1 RC when this triggers to extend the burst to 15 feet and deal 1d4 fire damage to hostile creatures in that area.

Prerequisite: Oracle 10; selected Curse:Burning Blood

### False Life

#### False Life — Boon III

**Level 10 · automatic for selected Curse · oracle.supplement.false-life.false-life-boon-iii**

| Activation | Cost | Range / targets | Duration | Frequency |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Triggered when reduced to 0 HP | 0 | Self | Until end of next regular turn | 1/long rest |

1/long rest when reduced to 0 HP without instant death, remain conscious until the end of your next regular turn. During that turn choose either move up to your Speed, speak normally or activate one Revelation; you cannot do more than one of those options. If still at 0 afterward, become unconscious normally.

Prerequisite: Oracle 10; selected Curse:False Life

<details><summary>Preserved supplemental source wording</summary>

```json
[
  {
    "reference": "CURSES.ORACLE_CURSES[6]",
    "value": {
      "id": "false-life",
      "name": "False Life",
      "blurb": "Magic mistakes your living breath for death and answers accordingly",
      "limitations": [
        "Healing spells cast on you without a successful Insight check have a 50% chance to fail; magic reads you as corpse-cold",
        "Unless you suppress the curse, divination treats you as undead",
        "Clerics of life-deities may hesitate to aid you without proof that you still breathe"
      ],
      "boons": [
        {
          "level": 3,
          "name": "Curse Boon I",
          "description": "Advantage on saves vs death effects and negative energy. Once per long rest, when you would die, stabilize at 1 HP as your body feigns death perfectly."
        },
        {
          "level": 5,
          "name": "Curse Boon II",
          "description": "Feign death perfectly for 1 minute — undead ignore you unless attacked; you remain conscious but motionless. Once per combat."
        },
        {
          "level": 10,
          "name": "Curse Boon III",
          "description": "When reduced to 0 HP, remain conscious for 1 round before falling — you may move, speak, or cast one Revelation. Once per long rest."
        },
        {
          "level": 15,
          "name": "Curse Boon IV",
          "description": "Necrotic resilience: +10% DR vs necrotic and negative energy. Undead attackers have 10% chance to miss you entirely, sensing kinship."
        },
        {
          "level": 20,
          "name": "Curse Boon V",
          "description": "Once per day, return from death's threshold — after 1 round of appearing dead at 0 HP, rise at 25% max HP with immunity to necrotic for 2 rounds. Undead within 3 tiles treat you as kin until you act, and divination cast during your feigned death returns false results of your choosing."
        }
      ],
      "draft": true,
      "source": "Draft — Aethel lore, pending author review"
    }
  }
]
```

</details>

#### False Life — Boon V

**Level 20 · automatic for selected Curse · oracle.supplement.false-life.false-life-boon-v**

| Activation | Cost | Range / targets | Duration | Frequency |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Triggered at 0 HP | 0 | Self; undead within 15 feet | Rise next turn; immunity two rounds | 1/long rest |

1/long rest when you are at 0 HP but not destroyed outright, appear dead until the start of your next regular turn. During that interval you are incapacitated. At the start of your next turn rise with floor(max HP/4), minimum 1, and gain necrotic immunity for 2 rounds. Undead within 15 feet treat you as non-hostile until you damage, control or overtly oppose them. Divination during the apparent-death interval returns one false but plausible state-description you choose; it cannot fabricate events outside your own condition.

Prerequisite: Oracle 20; selected Curse:False Life
