### Shattered Mind

#### Shattered Mind — Boon III

**Level 10 · automatic for selected Curse · oracle.supplement.shattered-mind.shattered-mind-boon-iii**

| Activation | Cost | Range / targets | Duration | Frequency |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Triggered when starting second sustained Revelation | Normal RC for second Revelation; normal upkeep | Your two active Revelations | Until end of next regular turn | 1/encounter; third effect ends both |

once per encounter when beginning a second sustained Revelation while one of your Revelations is already active, maintain both until the end of your next regular turn. Pay the second Revelation's normal RC cost but no additional special maintenance charge; normal concentration saves threaten both. At the end of the extension choose one sustained effect to end; starting a third ends the previous two immediately.

Prerequisite: Oracle 10; selected Curse:Shattered Mind

<details><summary>Preserved supplemental source wording</summary>

```json
[
  {
    "reference": "CURSES.ORACLE_CURSES[7]",
    "value": {
      "id": "shattered-mind",
      "name": "Shattered Mind",
      "blurb": "Your thoughts divide into competing fragments that act before they agree",
      "limitations": [
        "Concentration checks are at −2 as competing thoughts contend for control",
        "In tense social scenes, make a Wisdom save or speak a prophetic fragment aloud on an impulse not wholly your own",
        "After a long rest, make a Wisdom save or wake with fragmented memories of others' dreams"
      ],
      "boons": [
        {
          "level": 3,
          "name": "Curse Boon I",
          "description": "Once per combat, take a bonus move or activate one Tier I Revelation as a bonus action (not both a full action and bonus action in the same turn). The fragment acting is not wholly yours — observers may hear you answer questions you were never asked."
        },
        {
          "level": 5,
          "name": "Curse Boon II",
          "description": "Fragmented focus: +2 initiative; immune to being stunned. Your initiative roll may be made twice — take the higher result."
        },
        {
          "level": 10,
          "name": "Curse Boon III",
          "description": "Split thought: maintain two Revelation effects simultaneously for 1 round without paying double charges. Once per combat."
        },
        {
          "level": 15,
          "name": "Curse Boon IV",
          "description": "Mind echo: reroll one failed Intelligence or Wisdom check per short rest. Prophetic fragments grant +2 to Insight when reading motives."
        },
        {
          "level": 20,
          "name": "Curse Boon V",
          "description": "Once per day, act twice in a single round — second action may be a Revelation or move only. Each action uses a different fragment of your mind."
        }
      ],
      "draft": true,
      "source": "Draft — Aethel lore, pending author review"
    }
  }
]
```

### Consumed

#### Consumed — Boon III

**Level 10 · automatic for selected Curse · oracle.supplement.consumed.consumed-boon-iii**

| Activation | Cost | Range / targets | Duration | Frequency |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Triggered on weapon/touch hit | 0 | One creature hit | Instantaneous | Once/own turn |

once on each of your turns add +1d4 theme damage to one weapon or touch attack hit.

Prerequisite: Oracle 10; selected Curse:Consumed

<details><summary>Preserved supplemental source wording</summary>

```json
[
  {
    "reference": "CURSES.ORACLE_CURSES[8]",
    "value": {
      "id": "consumed",
      "name": "Consumed",
      "blurb": "Your Mystery remakes your body until its element shows through",
      "limitations": [
        "As you gain levels, your appearance shifts toward your Mystery's theme: flame hair, starlit eyes, bone protrusions, and the like",
        "Some civilized NPCs react with fear or awe; concealing the change in formal courts requires ongoing effort",
        "As your body changes, armor and gear must be refitted periodically (roleplay / downtime cost)"
      ],
      "boons": [
        {
          "level": 3,
          "name": "Curse Boon I",
          "description": "Gain +5% resistance aligned with your Mystery's element or theme (fire, necrotic, force, psychic, etc.). Once per day, suppress visible transformation for 1 hour in civilized areas — the Mystery still leaks through divination."
        },
        {
          "level": 5,
          "name": "Curse Boon II",

        },
        {
          "level": 10,
          "name": "Curse Boon III",
          "description": "Mystery infusion: weapon and touch attacks deal +1d4 thematic damage matching your Mystery. Revelation potency visibly bleeds into your strikes."
        },
        {
          "level": 15,
          "name": "Curse Boon IV",
          "description": "Partial transformation: ignore difficult terrain tied to your Mystery (fire walks, shadow steps, temporal stutter, etc.). Allies who follow within 1 tile during the same round may treat that terrain as normal ground until your next turn."
        },
        {
          "level": 20,
          "name": "Curse Boon V",
          "description": "Once per day, fully embody your Mystery's element for 3 rounds — immunity to that damage type, +2 effective Revelation potency, and visible manifestation (aura, wings of flame, star corona, etc.). Allies within 2 tiles gain +1 AC as your transformation steadies the battlefield; suppression is impossible while the embodiment lasts."
        }
      ],

    }
  }
]
```

### Fate-Bound

#### Fate-Bound — Boon III

**Level 10 · automatic for selected Curse · oracle.supplement.fate-bound.fate-bound-boon-iii**

| Activation | Cost | Range / targets | Duration | Frequency |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Triggered on ally’s natural 1 save | 0 | One ally within 25 feet and yourself | Immediate transfer; original effect duration | 1/long rest |

1/long rest when an ally within 25 feet rolls a natural 1 on a saving throw, you may transfer that failed save to yourself if the effect could legally affect you. The ally treats its save as successful; you suffer the failed-save effect as though you were the target, without another save. If the effect cannot legally affect you, the transfer cannot be used.

Prerequisite: Oracle 10; selected Curse:Fate-Bound

<details><summary>Preserved supplemental source wording</summary>

```json
[
  {
    "reference": "CURSES.ORACLE_CURSES[9]",
    "value": {
      "id": "fate-bound",
      "name": "Fate-Bound",
      "blurb": "Foreknowledge marks your path, but each omen narrows the road ahead",
      "limitations": [
        "You cannot voluntarily break a sworn oath or prophecy without losing 1 Revelation Charge and suffering psychic backlash",
        "Major life choices may feel predetermined once you have seen the threads that lead to them",
        "Trying to flee a foreseen disaster without confronting it imposes −2 to saves until you face the omen"
      ],
      "boons": [
        {
          "level": 3,
          "name": "Curse Boon I",
          "description": "Once per day, glimpse one likely outcome before a party decision — the ally acting gains +2 to the related check or save. You must speak the omen aloud; allies who heard it retain the bonus even if the party chooses a different path."
        },
        {
          "level": 5,
          "name": "Curse Boon II",
          "description": "Fated dodge: once per combat, force an attack against you to reroll as fate rewinds the strike. You must declare after the roll but before damage."
        },
        {
          "level": 10,
          "name": "Curse Boon III",
          "description": "When an ally within 5 tiles would critically fail a save, you may accept the failure on yourself instead (once per long rest). The thread binds to you."
        },
        {
          "level": 15,
          "name": "Curse Boon IV",
          "description": "Thread of destiny: +2 to saves vs death, fate-altering magic, and effects that would remove you from a prophesied path. Once per long rest, reroll a failed death save as the Concord refuses to cut your thread early."
        },
        {
          "level": 20,
          "name": "Curse Boon V",
          "description": "Once per day, declare a prophetic outcome — one ally within 6 tiles auto-succeeds their next save or attack as the Concord enforces the written thread. You must describe the outcome aloud before the roll; if the prophecy fails to come true by end of combat, you lose 1 Revelation Charge as the thread frays."
        }
      ],
      "draft": true,
      "source": "Draft — Aethel lore, pending author review"
    }
  }
]
```

</details>

### Ancestor

#### Ancestral Whispers

**Level 12 · automatic for chosen Mystery · oracle.supplement.ancestor.ancestral-whispers**

| Activation | Cost | Range / targets | Duration | Frequency |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Passive | 0 RC | Self | Permanent | Knowledge benefits always; consultation 1/long rest |

Gain advantage on History checks and you cannot be Surprised while conscious. Once per long rest, spend 1 minute consulting your ancestors about one specific person, place, object or course of action. The Director provides one truthful cryptic clue drawn from knowledge the ancestral spirits could reasonably possess; they do not reveal unknowable future decisions.

Prerequisite: Oracle 12; Mystery: Ancestor

```json
  }
]
```

#### Ancestor Revelation Mastery

**Level 15 · choose one eligible enhancement at Oracle 15 · oracle.supplement.ancestor.ancestor-revelation-mastery**

| Activation | Cost | Range / targets | Duration | Frequency |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Passive | 0 RC | One eligible known Ancestor Revelation | Permanent | Choose one upgrade at Oracle 15 |

Choose exactly one mastery for a Revelation you know. Enhanced Echo Voice: when Echo Voice triggers, you may spend 1 additional RC to let up to M qualifying allies within 5 tiles each reroll one failed roll caused by the same event, using +2. Enhanced Spirit Walk: Spirit Walk lasts until the end of your next regular turn, but you still may activate at most one additional Revelation during the extended walk. Enhanced Bloodline Convergence: remove its post-effect suffering Revelation backlash drawback.

Prerequisite: Oracle 15; Mystery: Ancestor; Revelation Mastery

```json
      "Enhanced Bloodline Convergence: no longer causes Exhaustion"
    ]
  }
]
```

</details>

#### Ancestral Confluence

**Level 18 · automatic for chosen Mystery · oracle.supplement.ancestor.ancestral-confluence**

| Activation | Cost | Range / targets | Duration | Frequency |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Triggered | 0 RC for chosen Revelation | One known Ancestor Revelation | Chosen Revelation plus cost reduction until end of your next regular turn | 1/long rest |

When activating a known Ancestor Revelation, reduce its RC cost to 0. Until the end of your next regular turn, your other Ancestor Revelations cost 1 less RC, minimum 0. A Revelation can receive this reduction once per activation and no free or reduced activation can recursively restore RC from its own copied effects.

Prerequisite: Oracle 18; Mystery: Ancestor

#### Echo Crowned

**Level 20 · automatic for chosen Mystery · oracle.supplement.ancestor.echo-crowned**

| Activation | Cost | Range / targets | Duration | Frequency |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Passive and Bonus Action | 0 RC to maintain spirits; Revelation costs normally unless stated | Self; death prevention ally within 5 tiles | Permanent spirits; activated effects instantaneous | Double-cast 1/long rest; each spirit may be sacrificed once per long rest |

Maintain three named ancestral Echoes: Offense grants +1 on your first Revelation attack each round; Defense reduces the first damage you take each round by S; Utility grants advantage on one History, Insight or Perception check per round. Once per long rest, when you activate a known Revelation, you may pay its RC cost twice and resolve it twice as part of the same action against different legal targets; copied resolution cannot duplicate resource or action triggers. You may sacrifice one currently available Echo as a Reaction when an ally within 5 tiles would be reduced to 0 HP to leave that ally at 1 HP instead, or as a Bonus Action to restore your RC to maximum. A sacrificed Echo provides no passive benefit until your next long rest.

Prerequisite: Oracle 20; Mystery: Ancestor

```json
    }
  }
]
```

Gain advantage on Perception and initiative rolls. Once per long rest when a visible enemy declares an attack against you or an ally within 5 tiles, use your Reaction before the roll; you and one ally within 5 tiles gain +2 AC against that enemy's next attack before the end of its current turn.

Enhanced Divine Fury: once per turn during Divine Fury, a third weapon or touch attack you make that turn may receive the Fury bonus damage, but the feature creates no additional attack beyond its printed Bonus Action attack. Enhanced Fury of the Host: remove its suffering Revelation backlash drawback.

#### Avatar of the War Chorus

**Level 20 · automatic for chosen Mystery · oracle.supplement.battle.avatar-of-the-war-chorus**

| Activation | Cost | Range / targets | Duration | Frequency |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Bonus Action | 0 RC | Self and allies within 5 tiles | 3 rounds | 1/long rest |

Manifest three banners for 3 rounds. Offense Banner: once on each of your turns add 2 S damage to one weapon or Revelation damage roll. Defense Banner: gain +2 AC; allies within 5 tiles reduce first damage each round by 1. Rally Banner: once per round one ally within 5 tiles gains +1 on an attack roll made during its turn. Your known Tier I Battle Revelations cost 0 RC while all three banners remain. You may sacrifice one banner as a Reaction to leave an ally within 5 tiles at 1 HP instead of 0, or as a Bonus Action to restore your RC to maximum; that banner's benefit ends immediately. When the 3-round effect ends naturally, regain 2 RC, never above maximum. The Final grants no more than one additional full Action per creature per round.

Prerequisite: Oracle 20; Mystery: Battle

#### Ledger Unbound

**Level 20 · automatic for chosen Mystery · oracle.supplement.bones.ledger-unbound**

| Activation | Cost | Range / targets | Duration | Frequency |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Action | 0 RC | Enemies and up to 3 eligible corpses within 5 tiles | Damage instantaneous; bone servants 3 rounds | 1/long rest |

Choose up to three enemies in range. Roll 4 S d8+M once; each target takes necrotic damage equal to the lesser of that roll or one-quarter its maximum HP, with a Constitution save for half. This blast cannot reduce HP below 1. Raise up to three nonsummoned corpses as 1-HP bone servants for three rounds. Once per round spend your shared companion Bonus Action to move and attack with ONE servant using your Revelation attack bonus for 1d8+S necrotic damage. Alternatively, your Reaction may sacrifice one servant to intercept a single-target attack against you; no attack is made by that servant that round. Servants grant no XP, loot, harvestable corpses or RC. For three rounds, up to three undead you already control add S damage to their one shared commanded attack and reduce the first damage they take each round by 1. No extra creature turns or command budgets are created.

Prerequisite: Oracle 20; Mystery: Bones

#### Stellar Oracle

| Activation | Cost | Range / targets | Duration | Frequency |

| Passive and Ritual | 0 RC | Self; one party decision | Permanent; divination after 10 minutes | Skill benefit always; guidance 1/long rest |

Gain advantage on checks involving astronomy, astrology and navigation. Once per long rest spend 10 minutes reading the sky or a divination focus concerning one specific party decision. The Director supplies one truthful but cryptic omen about a likely consequence known to the Celestial Concord. It cannot dictate another player's future decision or guarantee an outcome.

Prerequisite: Oracle 12; Mystery: Stars

Enhanced Echo Strike: its copied damage equals the triggering attack's full unmodified rolled damage dice total instead of half; it still copies no modifiers, conditions, resources or proc triggers. Enhanced Rewind Wound: may target yourself. #### Time Prophecy

**Level 18 · automatic for chosen Mystery · oracle.supplement.time.time-prophecy**

| Activation | Cost | Range / targets | Duration | Frequency |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Triggered | 0 RC for chosen Revelation | One known Time Revelation; allies within 5 tiles | Chosen Revelation; temporal benefit until end of your next regular turn | 1/long rest |

Activate one known Time Revelation for 0 RC. After it resolves, move your initiative position to immediately before the next hostile creature that has not yet acted this round; if all hostiles have acted, remain in your current position and act first among tied initiative positions next round. This never grants an immediate extra normal turn. Until the end of your next regular turn, allies within 5 tiles gain +2 on initiative-order comparisons and each may reroll one failed saving throw once, using the new result. These rerolls refund no resources or previously resolved consequences.

Prerequisite: Oracle 18; Mystery: Time

```json
]
```

#### The Spiral Opens

**Level 20 · automatic for chosen Mystery · oracle.supplement.time.the-spiral-opens**

| Activation | Cost | Range / targets | Duration | Frequency |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Action | 0 RC | Self; one known beneficial Time Revelation or one enemy within 5 tiles | 3 rounds | 1/long rest |

For 3 rounds, at the start of each of your regular turns choose ONE temporal option, each usable at most once during this Final. ECHO REVELATION: repeat only the immediate numeric damage, healing, movement or buff component of one beneficial Time Revelation you personally resolved during the previous round, paying 0 RC; this copy is not a new Revelation cast, copies no resource restoration, action grant, hard control, condition trigger, Prophecy Trigger or proc. FORCED ECHO: choose one non-player enemy within 5 tiles and one ordinary weapon attack or non-resource movement action it took during its previous turn. The enemy makes a Wisdom save. On failure, during its next turn it must repeat that action pattern if legally possible, but it chooses all legal targets and paths; it spends no limited resources and cannot be forced to harm itself. A player-controlled creature instead, on a failed save, loses its Reaction and must choose either repeat one ordinary non-resource action type from its prior turn or have Speed reduced to 0 until turn end. Allies retain memory of all temporal echoes. This Final never creates more than one additional full Action for any creature in a round. There are only two options in this Final; after using both once, remaining rounds provide only the printed temporal presence, without another echo. An echoed healing amount also pays 1 RC or consumes an unused per-rest healing use; a temporal copy cannot create an uncosted healing reserve.

Prerequisite: Oracle 20; Mystery: Time

```json
    }
  }
]
```

## Source appendix and open balance questions

The complete original Oracle book section, including lore, examples and alternate progression, is preserved in the [source evidence JSON](../../../data/playtest/class_sources/oracle.json). The supplemental source tables are [preserved separately](../../../data/playtest/class_sources/supplements.json). Do not stack older level-table features on top of this draft.

- Does the provisional RC cap schedule 3→4→5→6→7, combined with short-rest recovery, Prophecy Triggers and the level-11 encounter-start floor, sustain Revelations without making charge management irrelevant?
- Do Curse limitations remain meaningful after their Boons accumulate, especially for Spiral-Touched, Wound Eater, Shattered Mind and False Life, without producing excessive involuntary turn loss or noninteractive penalties?
- Do the shared hard-control and additional-Action caps adequately contain Time, Tongues, Shattered Mind and other future Mystery interactions while preserving each Curse's identity?

A complete card specification is not balance approval. Record actual play results, particularly resource exhaustion, condition locks, survival and how often choices become unusable.
